KOR_review_offer_category = {
	KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_accept_USA = {
		icon = GFX_decision_relation
		cost = 0
		allowed = {
			original_tag = USA
		}
		visible = {
			tag = USA
			KOR = {
				has_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_pending
				NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_df_foreign_purchase_board }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_assent_USA }
		}
		available = {
			KOR = {
				KOR_review_valid_df_foreign_purchase_board = yes
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_review_if_accepted
				custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_country_KOR
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = mdcw_infantry_weapons_1955 amount = 600 producer = KOR }
				set_temp_variable = { KOR_review_preview_amount = KOR.gdp_total }
				multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_review_preview_amount = 0.0012 }
				custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_flow_KOR_USA_once_out
				custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_country_USA
				custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_review_equipment_df_foreign_purchase_board_USA
				set_temp_variable = { KOR_review_preview_amount = KOR.gdp_total }
				multiply_temp_variable = { KOR_review_preview_amount = 0.0012 }
				custom_effect_tooltip = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_flow_USA_KOR_once_in
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						tag = USA
						KOR = {
							has_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_pending
							NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_signed_df_foreign_purchase_board }
						}
						NOT = { has_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_assent_USA }
						KOR = {
							KOR_review_valid_df_foreign_purchase_board = yes
						}
					}
					set_country_flag = KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_assent_USA
					KOR = {
						KOR_review_offer_df_foreign_purchase_board_settle = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 2
		}
	}
